# Claim Ledger — Witnessed Difference Programme

**Canonical manuscript:** witnessed_difference_programme.md

**Date:** 2026-10-16

**Version:** 2.4.4

**Rule:** this is the only live claim ledger for the consolidated programme.

Row IDs are stable identifiers, not locations. In particular, `CH19-O01`–
`CH19-O04`, `CH11-O01`, and `CH04-O01` retain their names after scoped
resolution; the letter `O` is not their current status. Read the mathematical status column.
Open obligations live in register rows `GEN-WDP-O01`–`GEN-WDP-O05` in
[the bridge register](docs/genesis_bridge_register.md) and in
[the outstanding-derivations register](docs/five_outstanding_derivations.md).

## Evidence grammar

The mathematical status and statement in every row define the canonical register of the monograph. The separate reproduction table below records formalization scope and execution/reproduction status. A proof, an exact Lean declaration, and a successful paired replay answer different questions.

- **P** means the canonical prose location states, defines, proves, scopes, or refuses the row exactly as its status says. For an open claim or nonclaim, P is not a proof.
- **C** means the row has an explicit executable fixture, finite witness, or refusal/scope binding in manifests/claim_code_map.json. It never promotes an open claim.
- **L:exact(name)** means the cited Lean declaration matches the canonical row at the stated finite scope, as recorded in lean4/claim_map.md. Related but narrower Lean declarations are not credited as exact bindings.
- **L:partial(name)** names a mechanized milestone narrower than the canonical row. It supplies no whole-row Lean credit.
- **R** is admitted only through an inline REF-* key and its consultation boundary in docs/references.md. A citation does not prove a new WDP claim.

- **N** records production native crate validation described in docs/derivation_review.md; it is not a standalone Python binding or an extension of the historical Lean census.
- **Mathematical status** describes the written argument at the row's stated hypotheses. A missing executable binding does not turn a completed argument into an open claim.
- **Formalization scope** is `EXACT_DECLARATION`, `PARTIAL_DECLARATION`, or `NONE_BOUND`, according to the L tags above. A named declaration is not a fresh compilation receipt.
- **Execution/reproduction status** retains the strict paired gate: an exact Lean statement must elaborate without admitted proofs and its bound Python check must run successfully on the recorded sources. `PAIRED_REPLAY_PENDING` records an unmet reproduction gate, not a mathematical open problem. `RECORDED_PAIRED_PASS_2026-10-09` refers only to the nine rows listed in that dated reproduction entry; this editorial pass does not renew that receipt. `NOT_APPLICABLE` marks definitions, nonclaims, and synthesis summaries, not a test pass.
- **Binding completeness** records whether both an exact Lean declaration and an explicit Python binding are present. `COMPLETE` is a prerequisite for paired reproduction, never evidence that either was executed.

Coverage, mutation sensitivity, notebook execution, and artifact hashes are verification receipts, not mathematical or physical truth. Conceptual artwork is never evidence.

The 2026-10-13 statement corrections below synchronize the live register with
the manuscript. They make the fresh-form naming convention, real scalar dual
space and free, repeatable table-formation premises explicit. The dated
formalization/reproduction snapshot and historical crosswalk retain their
recorded scope; no new compilation or paired pass is inferred from this update.

| WDP-CH10-D06 | DEFINITION | [#chapter-10](witnessed_difference_programme.md#chapter-10) | P; C | A quotient observer is a linear report map R on C1 with R d0=0; it enters the layered observer only as a presentation map and as the evaluator cost Phi_O(y)=inf{Phi1(r): O(r)=y}, supplying no memory, policy, or authority layer. |

| WDP-CH10-P03 | FINITE_PROPOSITION | [#chapter-10](witnessed_difference_programme.md#chapter-10) | P; C; L:exact(grammar_resolution_bound) | A deterministic report map from a declared finite set of core classes into a finite grammar G exposes at most the size of G visible classes, and forces two distinct core classes to share a report when the core set is larger than G. |

| WDP-CH18-P01 | FINITE_PROPOSITION | [#chapter-18](witnessed_difference_programme.md#chapter-18) | P; C; R | The convex set with extreme points (1,0), (0,1), (-1,0), (0,-1) is not a simplex because its centre equals both one half of v1 plus one half of v3 and one half of v2 plus one half of v4, so nonsimpliciality alone is not a quantum discriminator. |

| WDP-CH18-P03 | FINITE_PROPOSITION | [#chapter-18](witnessed_difference_programme.md#chapter-18) | P; C; L:exact(signed_weights_break_the_corner) | With signed weights, the matrix [[2,1],[-1,0]] and its inverse [[0,-1],[1,2]] both preserve total mass, the first is not a permutation matrix, and it sends (1,0) to (2,-1), so pointwise nonnegativity is what forces permutations in CH18-P02. |
| WDP-CH18-P04 | FINITE_PROPOSITION | [#chapter-18](witnessed_difference_programme.md#chapter-18) | P; C; L:exact(unitary_package_is_classical) | Every matrix U over the Gaussian integers with U*U = I is monomial, with entries 1, -1, i or -i in rows given by one permutation, so the phase-blind readout moves by that same permutation and no such U separates two vectors with equal readout. |
| WDP-CH18-P05 | FINITE_PROPOSITION | [#chapter-18](witnessed_difference_programme.md#chapter-18) | P; C; L:exact(classical_corner_ends) | The unnormalized Hadamard matrix satisfies H*H = 2I and separates a readout fiber, which no unitary over `Z[i]` can do, while unitaries with entries in the finite localizations `Z[1/5]` and `Z[i][1/2]` separate readout fibers, and the second squares to the toggle. |
| WDP-CH18-P06 | FINITE_PROPOSITION | [#chapter-18](witnessed_difference_programme.md#chapter-18) | P | The eighteen vectors of the section 18.4 table, with coordinates in {-1,0,1}, form nine contexts of four pairwise orthogonal vectors of Q^4 in which no two vectors are parallel and every vector lies in exactly two contexts, and no map from the vectors to F2 gives every context the sum 1, because the nine context equations add to 0 = 1 in F2; in particular no {0,1}-valuation puts exactly one 1 in every context. |
| WDP-CH18-P07 | FINITE_PROPOSITION | [#chapter-18](witnessed_difference_programme.md#chapter-18) | P; C; L:exact(stochastic_updates_are_mixtures_of_functions) | Over a finite report carrier the state-preserving linear updates are the column-stochastic matrices, closed under composition and containing the identity; those with all entries 0 or 1 are exactly the updates induced by functions of reports, and every stochastic update is a mixture of function updates with weights the products over columns j of T(f(j),j), which are nonnegative and sum to one. |
| WDP-CH18-P08 | FINITE_PROPOSITION | [#chapter-18](witnessed_difference_programme.md#chapter-18) | P; L:exact(classical_package_and_sharpness) | For a classical instrument, a family of nonnegative updates whose sum is column-stochastic, and a nonnegative state, the law of an outcome word is nonnegative, the laws of the words of each length add up to the mass, and the law is consistent under dropping the last outcome; every noncontextual model on the CHSH cover has CHSH value at most 2; and without nonnegative weights the signed shear of CH18-P03 has a negative entry and is not a permutation, while without joint runs the PR box of CH19-P05 has CHSH value 4 and no nonnegative global section. |

The empirical image retains only the previously stated outcome information. |
| WDP-CH19-P01 | FINITE_PROPOSITION | [#chapter-19](witnessed_difference_programme.md#chapter-19) | P; C; R | If the amplitudes of finitely many alternatives add and the readout is P(S)=B(A(S),A(S)) for a declared biadditive B, the alternating Sorkin sum vanishes for every set of at least three alternatives while I_2 equals B(a,b)+B(b,a), so vanishing higher-order interference is a consequence of the declared readout and not a derivation of it. |
| WDP-CH19-P02 | FINITE_PROPOSITION | [#chapter-19](witnessed_difference_programme.md#chapter-19) | P; L:exact(measurement_packet_adapter) | On a declared cover the adapter sends a finite table of measurement packets to its frequencies, refuses it with the evidence when a context has no runs or when two contexts disagree on their overlap, and otherwise returns an empirical model, namely the model m exactly when no context is empty and m is the table of frequencies. |
| WDP-CH19-P03 | FINITE_PROPOSITION | [#chapter-19](witnessed_difference_programme.md#chapter-19) | P; L:exact(what_survives_the_adapter) | A section has positive weight in the image exactly when a recorded run reports it; the image depends only on the reported outcomes, and two nonempty tables agree in a context exactly when their outcome counts there are proportional, so no observer or evaluator field survives the map except through the outcome it reports, and fields the readout never reads (trace, evaluator, calibration chain, uncertainties, unit, scale, provenance) do not affect it. |
| WDP-CH19-P04 | FINITE_PROPOSITION | [#chapter-19](witnessed_difference_programme.md#chapter-19) | P; L:exact(every_model_is_a_packet_image) | If every outcome of every measurement is reported by some admissible packet, arbitrary tuples of those packets may form context runs, and each run may be repeated any finite number of times, every empirical model with rational weights on the declared cover is the image of a finite table. Under this table-formation rule the packet type neither forces nor excludes contextuality; formal table realization does not establish physical availability of the joint experiment. |
| WDP-CH19-P05 | FINITE_PROPOSITION | [#chapter-19](witnessed_difference_programme.md#chapter-19) | P; L:exact(contextuality_is_a_missing_global_table) | Under CH19-P04 and the same free, repeatable tuple-formation rule for all measurements at once, an empirical model with rational weights on a declared finite cover has a nonnegative rational global section exactly when it is the image of a table of global runs. The written rational feasible-point argument also identifies this with existence of a nonnegative real global section; that extension is not part of the exact Lean binding. On the CHSH cover every global table has CHSH value at most 2, the PR box has a signed global section with a negative weight and no nonnegative one, and a finite table of accepted packets for a concrete profile has the PR box as its image, with CHSH value 4 and strong contextuality. This is formal table realization, not physical availability of a PR experiment. |
| WDP-CH19-P06 | FINITE_PROPOSITION | [#chapter-19](witnessed_difference_programme.md#chapter-19) | P; L:exact(tolerant_adapter_signature) | For contexts i and j let D_ij be the l1 distance between the overlap marginals of their frequency tables; the tolerant adapter accepts a table exactly when no context is empty and every D_ij is at most epsilon, returning the frequencies with a certificate that every D_ij is at most epsilon and otherwise naming the empty context or a pair with D_ij above epsilon; at epsilon 0 it accepts exactly the tables of CH19-P02 and returns the same frequencies, acceptance is monotone in epsilon, and if every context is within epsilon in l1 of a compatible model then every D_ij is at most 2 epsilon. |
| WDP-CH19-P07 | FINITE_PROPOSITION | [#chapter-19](witnessed_difference_programme.md#chapter-19) | P; L:exact(two_context_tolerance_iff) | On a cover with two contexts and a nonempty outcome set, replacing each overlap marginal by the average of the two and rescaling the conditional weights gives an empirical model at l1 distance exactly D/2 from each table, where D is the signalling defect, so a compatible model within epsilon of both tables exists exactly when D is at most 2 epsilon. |
| WDP-CH19-O02 | CONDITIONAL_RESULT | [#chapter-19](witnessed_difference_programme.md#chapter-19) | P; C; N; L:partial(ReconstructionClassification) | Declaring positive finite Hilbert density states, local Hermitian dichotomic observables and trace pairing on the tensor composite gives the exact CHSH bound $S^2\le8$, excluding PR. Commuting complete local matrix units yield the tensor-factor representation up to a multiplicity space, removed by the stated dimension condition. The native instrument and observer adapters exhibit consistency with the finite architecture. These assumptions are inputs; CP alone does not supply them. |
| WDP-CH19-P08 | FINITE_PROPOSITION | [#chapter-19](witnessed_difference_programme.md#chapter-19) | P; L:exact(locallyRecoverable_iff_injective) | For nonempty finite carriers A and B, a composite tagging c from A x B has decoders for both local reports exactly when c is injective, and the tagging that forgets the left report has no decoder for the left factor when A has two elements. |
| WDP-CH19-P09 | FINITE_PROPOSITION | [#chapter-19](witnessed_difference_programme.md#chapter-19) | P; L:exact(noSignalling_right) | On the Cartesian composite a classical instrument acting on one factor, after summing over all outcomes, does not change the marginal of the other factor, so the marginal is the same for any two instruments on the first factor, and a presentation map on one factor lifts to the composite and commutes with marginalization. |
| WDP-CH19-P10 | FINITE_PROPOSITION | [#chapter-19](witnessed_difference_programme.md#chapter-19) | P; L:exact(composite_state_iff_joint_distribution) | For finite classical carriers a bilinear form on effect vectors that is nonnegative on product effects and normalized is exactly a joint distribution on the Cartesian product, uniquely, and on the CHSH product cover the PR box is compatible on every overlap yet not noncontextual with CHSH value 4, so compatibility on overlaps does not force the local composite. |
| WDP-CH19-O03 | CONDITIONAL_RESULT | [#chapter-19](witnessed_difference_programme.md#chapter-19) | P; C; N; L:partial(RationalPhaseBoundary) | A declared continuous character $\chi_\theta(t)=e^{i\theta t}$ maps real cochains into complex path amplitudes and descends under gauge to phase-blind operational readouts; its cycle-period lattice and anchor dependence are explicit. An independent exact rational-turn/cyclotomic branch is implemented. Declared effect/instrument pairing supplies a conditional operational model, while normalized quadratic and quartic readouts prove nonselection by phase invariance. No Born rule or faithful real phase embedding is inferred. |
| WDP-CH19-P11 | FINITE_PROPOSITION | [#chapter-19](witnessed_difference_programme.md#chapter-19) | P; L:exact(common_phase_iff) | Over the Gaussian rationals two pairs of amplitudes with the first nonzero differ by one common unit-norm phase exactly when their norms and their cross terms agree, so a phase-blind readout of two alternatives depends only on the two magnitudes and the phase between them; for two walks with common endpoints the cross term is the product of the magnitudes times the loop holonomy, which is unchanged when an integer cochain changes by a coboundary, and the quadratic and quartic readouts are both phase-blind and differ. |
| WDP-CH19-P12 | FINITE_PROPOSITION | [#chapter-19](witnessed_difference_programme.md#chapter-19) | P; L:exact(exponentiation_obstruction) | The rational phase (3+4i)/5 has no square root among the unit-norm Gaussian rationals, so no group homomorphism from the rationals under addition to those elements sends 1 to it; integer exponents compose, its positive powers are never 1, and integer cochains exponentiate to phases with coboundaries going to gauge transformations. |
| WDP-CH19-N01 | NONCLAIM | [#chapter-19](witnessed_difference_programme.md#chapter-19) | P | No uniqueness theorem for the quadratic Born rule is proved. |
| WDP-CH19-N02 | NONCLAIM | [#chapter-19](witnessed_difference_programme.md#chapter-19) | P; C | Phase, contextuality, and cancellation do not individually characterize quantum mechanics. |
| WDP-CH19-O04 | FINITE_PROPOSITION | [#chapter-19](witnessed_difference_programme.md#chapter-19) | P; C; N; L:partial(InstrumentRepeatability) | Grouped finite Gaussian-rational Kraus instruments preserve their declared probabilities, post-states and canonical dilation. On a common input/output carrier, outcome repeatability is equivalent to $K_{jb}K_{oa}=0$ for every $j\ne o,a,b$. Independent replay checks the complete registration, witness, accepted observation, indication, calibration enclosure and declared documentary hierarchy against actual inputs. Documentary traceability does not authenticate a physical standard or select the Born readout. |
| WDP-CH19-P13 | FINITE_PROPOSITION | [#chapter-19](witnessed_difference_programme.md#chapter-19) | P; L:partial(prob_eq_effect) | For a qubit over the Gaussian rationals, a state given as a finite list of nonnegative weights and vectors and a Kraus family with sum of K-dagger K equal to 1, the declared outcome probabilities equal the mixture trace of the effect against the state, are nonnegative and add to one, and the post-measurement state for a positive outcome is again such a state of trace one; the probabilities form an empirical model on the one-measurement cover, to which CH19-P04 applies. |
| WDP-CH19-P14 | FINITE_PROPOSITION | [#chapter-19](witnessed_difference_programme.md#chapter-19) | P; C; L:exact(povm_does_not_determine_the_instrument) | The Lueders instrument and the measure-and-prepare instrument that prepares (3/5,4/5) are both complete and have the same effects, hence the same outcome probabilities on every state, while the outcome 0 on the input (1,0) leaves (1,0) under one and (3/5,4/5) under the other, which are not parallel. |
| WDP-CH20-D01 | DEFINITION | [#chapter-20](witnessed_difference_programme.md#chapter-20) | P; C | An evaluator is complete for a finite query set Q relative to profile Pi when it assigns exactly one terminal status to every query and its proof or refutation evidence verifies. |
| WDP-CH20-O01 | FINITE_PROPOSITION | [#chapter-20](witnessed_difference_programme.md#chapter-20) | P; C; N | A declared finite Boolean semantic model with admission, policy and bounded independent evidence replay has five exclusive and exhaustive statuses; injective value-preserving profile adapters transport verified evidence and rerun target evaluation rather than assuming status invariance under changed policy or resources. |
| WDP-CH20-N01 | NONCLAIM | [#chapter-20](witnessed_difference_programme.md#chapter-20) | P; C | Relative completeness is not omniscience, a universal truth-decider, an escape from Goedelian limits, world creation, or cosmogenesis. |
| WDP-CH22-C01 | SYNTHESIS_CONCLUSION | [#chapter-22](witnessed_difference_programme.md#chapter-22) | P | Finite witnessed-difference systems can be organized as an explicit obstruction calculus whose conclusions carry typed assumption costs. |
<!-- CANONICAL-CLAIM-TABLE:END -->

## Scoped supplement evidence (Appendix H)

The first supplement supplied restricted instances. The subsequent native integration
discharged eight targets in Appendix I. Appendix J adds ten finite or conditional resolutions, including negative reconstruction/completion results. Appendix K completes CH10-O01 after recovery of every original source and source-specific semantic verification. All nineteen targets are resolved at their stated finite or conditional scopes. Stable IDs and the historical Lean census are retained.

| Supplement | Declared scope | Evidence | Remaining boundary |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| H.1 | Edge-calibrated finite occurrence chains and block-sum refinement | P; C: finite_adequacy.py | Physical calibration and unrestricted clock packets |
| H.2 | Full finite Boolean algebras, atomic masses and common pushforward | P; C: finite_adequacy.py | Singleton uniqueness on coarse event algebras |
| H.3 | Subset inclusion site, union covers, unconstrained function reports | P; Rust source-harness checks | General observer categories and constrained reports |
| H.4 | Surjective kernel-compatible factorization and reflecting contextual units | P; L: seven checked RefinementFactorization theorems | Noncongruent-source completion and categorical initiality |
| H.5 | Real rational qubits; same-carrier unweighted U(1) Laplacians | P; Rust tests supplied, production build blocked | General channels, vector bundles and independently certified spectral gaps |
| H.6 | Finite Boolean tables, explicit policy/budget and injective model encoding | P; C: finite_profile_evaluator.py | Arbitrary semantic predicates and status invariance under changed policy |



## Preserver and generator supplement R12/R13

These are Chapter 19A supplement rows, outside the canonical 154-row table.
P denotes the written proof at the stated hypotheses, C a concrete executable
check, L an explicitly named mathematical formalization, and R a scoped
literature dependency. No evidence type promotes another automatically.
The [full proof](docs/derivations/certified_preserver_derivation.md) and
[replay guide](docs/derivation_evidence.md#certified-preservers-and-algebraic-generators)
give the hypotheses, commands and source bindings.

| Supplement ID | Statement and status | Evidence and limit |
|---|---|---|
| WDP-R12-P01 | FINITE_PROPOSITION: pure-ray readout is representative-independent, bounded and injective; the displayed finite probe family suffices | P; L:exact for representative independence, bounds and full-table injectivity in `CertifiedPreservers`. The additional \(d^2\)-probe reconstruction is written, not mechanized. |
| WDP-R12-O01 | CONDITIONAL_RESULT: every global orthogonality-preserving ray bijection, \(d\ge3\), has a projectively unique semilinear similarity representative | P: native coordinate proof. L:partial for rational scalar homomorphisms and concrete frame rigidity. C: full Gram identity and declared-frame agreement for a supplied representative, not classification of a sampled black-box map. REF-PRES-001 is positioning only. |
| WDP-R12-P02 | FINITE_PROPOSITION: equal multipliers leave the infinite rational unit-norm group, with the displayed unique parameter away from \(-1\) | P; L:exact for rational parametrization, its injectivity and inverse, the non-\(\mu_4\) counterexample, and equal-multiplier criterion. The separate torsion-subgroup proof remains written. |
| WDP-R12-P03 | CONDITIONAL_RESULT: positive rational norm realization is equivalent to even prime valuations at \(p\equiv3\pmod4\) | P; R:REF-NORM-001 for integer two squares. L:exact conditional reduction with the arithmetic equivalence explicitly assumed and the prime predicate defined. C: native factorization, submitted norms, signed valuation witnesses and exhaustion. |
| WDP-R12-O02 | CONDITIONAL_RESULT: unitary ray reachability is exactly equality of Gaussian norm classes for the supplied positive form | P; L:exact construction of an isometry and inverse, equal-norm transport and reachability iff a Gaussian norm. C: original-pair-bound positive and negative certificates; finite height-one census is bounded evidence only. |
| WDP-R12-P04 | FINITE_PROPOSITION: the specified rank-one Lüders branch has probability \(\tau\), with no conditional ray at zero | P; L:exact idempotence, adjoint, probability, zero-branch and complement-mass laws. C: submitted projector and original target replay. Neither scope proves an optimal operating cost. |
| WDP-R12-P05 | FINITE_PROPOSITION: full probability preservation in dimension two implies a projectively unique semisimilarity | P: orthogonal-basis/four-probe proof. No complete Lean classification claimed. |
| CM-W1 | COUNTERMODEL: a complement-closed six-ray permutation extends to a global orthogonality-preserving qubit bijection that changes probabilities | P for the extension argument; C in Rust/Python for every pair in the six-ray pocket. |
| WDP-R12-O03 | CONDITIONAL_RESULT: pointwise-readout Galois pair, finite dagger bicommutant, and sharp completion count on a declared linear defect carrier | P for all three typed laws and the noninjective-menu/trivial-stabilizer counterexample. Appendix T supplies the linear completion context. No new native bicommutant solver or blanket nonlinear-minimality claim. |
| WDP-R13-P01 | FINITE_PROPOSITION: every supplied \(K\)-linear dagger-compatible matrix derivation has a Hermitian commutator generator, unique modulo \(\mathbb QI\) | P: explicit matrix-unit proof. C: Python independently checks the submitted generator against the entire supplied basis table. No Lean inner-derivation theorem, Rust generator binding, or continuous flow is claimed. |

## Binding and promotion rules

1. The canonical WDP-CH claim subset of the manuscript, this ledger, and lean4/claim_map.md must each contain exactly the same 154 unique IDs. Later supplement IDs have separate evidence rows and do not alter that historical count.
2. manifests/claim_code_map.json is intentionally a subset: only executable finite witnesses and refusal/scope checks belong there. Unmapped claims receive no computational upgrade.
3. Only the thirty exact bindings in lean4/claim_map.md (twenty-nine theorem rows and one definition row) receive exact Lean credit. L:partial tags on open rows discharge nothing. Every other claim_map entry is a definition/open/nonclaim registry row or is explicitly narrower than the manuscript statement.
4. A finite census proves only its named carrier. A property test samples its generated domain. Neither supplies an unbounded theorem without a verified reduction.
5. External results require an inline REF-* key, a resolved record, and hypotheses compatible with the manuscript use. Metadata-only checks support positioning, not theorem import.
6. Hosted CI, publication, deposit, DOI minting, authorization, jurisdiction, and authority are separate external states. No row in this package confers them.

## Source custody and retired namespaces

The superseded source-paper bytes are identity-bound by
`source_provenance.json`, which records their original paths, sizes, and
SHA-256 values but is not a byte archive. All 44 input sources are tracked in the
Git repository and remain recoverable from Git history. Zero uncommitted source
files were required for archiving. The 48 tracked files of the sibling package
`006_primitive_axiomatics` (v2.10.0), absorbed on 2026-09-29, are bound in the
separate `absorbed_snapshots` block of the same file; that package also keeps
its own deposit record, and its claim IDs enter the crosswalk below as
historical occurrences without inherited status. Six genesis-alpha working
papers (012, 015, 003, 035, 020, 007 under `../../000_genesis_alpha_program/`)
were absorbed on the same date under the same rules, each with its own
snapshot block, and the remaining eight (004, 005, 006, 008, 009, 010, 011,
022) on 2026-09-30. Every absorbed genesis-alpha directory has since been
removed from the tree; the bytes remain at the recorded commits. Historical namespaces (including WD0–WD7 and local paper-specific
ledgers) are provenance labels only and have no live status unless mapped to
one canonical WDP-CHxx row. Source headers such as RELEASED, canonical, or
v1.0 are historical metadata rather than current evidence.

Scoped canonical results and enlarged open obligations have different domains.
The latter remain in `GEN-WDP-O01`–`GEN-WDP-O05` and the outstanding-derivations
register linked above. Updating a claim requires its manuscript statement,
status row, dependency entry and evidence bindings to agree; an identifier
does not supply a status or a location.

| 002_self_witnessing_and_the_awareness_boundary/claim_ledger.md::WD5-L1 | WD5-L1 | 002_self_witnessing_and_the_awareness_boundary/claim_ledger.md:172 | — | — | NOT_CARRIED | — | Historical source-path occurrence retained for provenance; no statement-level equivalence to a canonical row is asserted, and no historical status is inherited. | &#124; WD5-L1 &#124; ~~A nonempty witness set forces two report-distinguishable states, hence Nerode index $\geq 2$~~ &#124; WDP004-C2 &#124; **FALSE — refuted 2026-07-21.** A nonempty witness set forces two **profile**-distinguishable states; the Nerode index is a property of $r$ and of reachability from $s_{0}$, and $W(p,\rho)$ mentions neither. Explicit countermodel: $A_{2}$ with a constant report (paper §4.0(ii)). **Replacement statement, corrected by the repair pass 2026-07-21:** *if the **report refines** $p$ — i.e. $p(a) \neq p(b) \Rightarrow \exists\sigma.\, r(a,\sigma) \neq r(b,\sigma)$ — and $W$ is restricted to reachable states, then a nonempty witness set forces index $\geq 2$* — an R1-branch lemma, not a free one. **The first replacement said *"if $p$ factors the report"*, which is the converse and does not give the conclusion**: $W(p,\rho) \neq \varnothing$ delivers $p$-*distinct* states, so one needs $p$-distinctness to force report-distinctness, not the other way round. Same inversion as §4.0's R1 row, now fixed in both places &#124; Lean; also numerically. The refutation is enumerated (&#96;wd8_enum.py&#96;, check I) &#124; |
| 002_self_witnessing_and_the_awareness_boundary/claim_ledger.md::WD5-L2 | WD5-L2 | 002_self_witnessing_and_the_awareness_boundary/claim_ledger.md:173 | — | — | NOT_CARRIED | — | Historical source-path occurrence retained for provenance; no statement-level equivalence to a canonical row is asserted, and no historical status is inherited. | &#124; WD5-L2 &#124; For $\lvert S \rvert \leq 2$, $W(p,\rho) = \varnothing$ for all $p, \rho$ &#124; WDP004-C4 &#124; Looks routine — **and the 2026-07-21 enumeration found no obstacle**: $0/2$ pairs at $\lvert S\rvert{=}1$, $0/16$ at $\lvert S\rvert{=}2$, and $0$ at $\lvert S\rvert{=}2$ for $\lvert\Pi\rvert \in \{2,3,4\}$ &#124; Lean; exhaustively at $\lvert S \rvert = 2$. **Checked, not proved** — the enumeration fixes $\lvert C\rvert = 1$ and bounded $\lvert\Pi\rvert$ &#124; |

| 002_self_witnessing_and_the_awareness_boundary/claim_ledger.md::WD5-L6 | WD5-L6 | 002_self_witnessing_and_the_awareness_boundary/claim_ledger.md:177 | — | — | NOT_CARRIED | — | Historical source-path occurrence retained for provenance; no statement-level equivalence to a canonical row is asserted, and no historical status is inherited. | &#124; WD5-L6 &#124; ~~Reachable self-traces exceed $\lvert R \rvert^{k}$ for some finite packet with bounded window~~ &#124; WDP004-C10 (**T6b half only**) &#124; **MALFORMED — 2026-07-21.** A self-trace *is* an element of $R^{\leq k}$, so the count of reachable self-traces cannot exceed $\lvert R^{\leq k}\rvert$; the statement appears satisfiable only because the threshold $\lvert R\rvert^{k}$ is too small. **Replacement statement:** *reachable **states** exceed $\sum_{i=0}^{k}\lvert R\rvert^{i}$ for some finite packet with a bounded window* — that is the real pigeonhole hypothesis and it is not free, since a packet may have few reachable states. **Repair-pass scoping, 2026-07-21:** this lemma supports **T6b only**. Any language making it a *precondition* of the boxed universal — the producer pass's "the universal must become conditional" — is deleted: **T6a needs no support lemma at all**, being one line from A4 (paper §4.6a), so a malformed hypothesis here never threatened it &#124; Lean, **via &#96;PaperFOC000.L000_07_GrammarBound&#96;** (&#96;grammar_collision_of_card_lt&#96;) rather than a fresh proof; also numerically &#124; |

| 002_self_witnessing_and_the_awareness_boundary/claim_ledger.md::WDP004-C10 | WDP004-C10 | 002_self_witnessing_and_the_awareness_boundary/claim_ledger.md:138 | &#96;&#91;OPEN&#93;&#96; (both halves) | None. **Re-scoped 2026-07-21, then re-typed by the repair pass the same day.** Producer findings that **stand**: the bound is $\sum_{i=0}^{k}\lvert R\rvert^{i}$ not $\lvert R\rvert^{k}$ (enumerated: $3\neq2$ at $\lvert R\rvert{=}2,k{=}1$), and the printed hypothesis names *self-traces* where it means *reachable states* and is vacuous once the bound is corrected. **Producer finding WITHDRAWN:** *"the bare universal is **false** at $\lvert S_{\mathrm{obj}}\rvert = 1$"*. It is **not** false. That verdict evaluated the universal over **A1**-packets; over $\mathrm{A1}\wedge\mathrm{A2}\wedge\mathrm{A3}\wedge\mathrm{A4}$ packets — §3.2's "self-witnessing" — **A4 alone entails T6a in one line**: A4's witness $(x,y,c)$ has $p_{\mathrm{self}}(\rho(c)x)\neq p_{\mathrm{self}}(\rho(c)y)$, so $\rho(c)x \neq \rho(c)y$, so $x \neq y$, with $p_{\mathrm{self}}(x)=p_{\mathrm{self}}(y)$. At $\lvert S_{\mathrm{obj}}\rvert = 1$, $p_{\mathrm{self}}$ is injective, A4 **fails**, and such packets are not O3 — so they are not counterexamples. The "pigeonhole" withdrawal is narrowed to **T6a only**; T6b is a genuine pigeonhole | NOT_CARRIED | — | Historical source-path occurrence retained for provenance; no statement-level equivalence to a canonical row is asserted, and no historical status is inherited. | &#124; WDP004-C10 &#124; **T6a:** every O3 packet has a nonempty self-blind-spot set — *unconditionally*. **T6b:** the reachability-restricted form, over reachable states, with bound $\sum_{i=0}^{k}\lvert R\rvert^{i}$ &#124; &#96;&#91;OPEN&#93;&#96; (both halves) &#124; None. **Re-scoped 2026-07-21, then re-typed by the repair pass the same day.** Producer findings that **stand**: the bound is $\sum_{i=0}^{k}\lvert R\rvert^{i}$ not $\lvert R\rvert^{k}$ (enumerated: $3\neq2$ at $\lvert R\rvert{=}2,k{=}1$), and the printed hypothesis names *self-traces* where it means *reachable states* and is vacuous once the bound is corrected. **Producer finding WITHDRAWN:** *"the bare universal is **false** at $\lvert S_{\mathrm{obj}}\rvert = 1$"*. It is **not** false. That verdict evaluated the universal over **A1**-packets; over $\mathrm{A1}\wedge\mathrm{A2}\wedge\mathrm{A3}\wedge\mathrm{A4}$ packets — §3.2's "self-witnessing" — **A4 alone entails T6a in one line**: A4's witness $(x,y,c)$ has $p_{\mathrm{self}}(\rho(c)x)\neq p_{\mathrm{self}}(\rho(c)y)$, so $\rho(c)x \neq \rho(c)y$, so $x \neq y$, with $p_{\mathrm{self}}(x)=p_{\mathrm{self}}(y)$. At $\lvert S_{\mathrm{obj}}\rvert = 1$, $p_{\mathrm{self}}$ is injective, A4 **fails**, and such packets are not O3 — so they are not counterexamples. The "pigeonhole" withdrawal is narrowed to **T6a only**; T6b is a genuine pigeonhole &#124; **T6a:** write it as the one-line consequence of A4 that it is. **This entry records the derivation; it does not promote the row** — promotion is a separate reviewed act, the paper is an outline, and no proof has been written or checked here. **T6b:** PL-WDP004-6, restated — **derive from &#96;ENC-0774&#96;** (Lean module &#96;PaperFOC000.L000_07_GrammarBound&#96;, &#96;grammar_collision_of_card_lt&#96;), do not re-prove. **The ledger's earlier assessment "expected easiest row on the sheet" stays withdrawn**, but for the corrected reason: the row bundled a one-line theorem with a wrong bound, a vacuous hypothesis, and an open reachability claim &#124; |

Mechanized: the pigeonhole step and the List-side capacity implication. Prose only: the $\Sigma$-sum **count**. &#96;ENC-0774&#96;'s Lean-obligation table is honest **in scope** — it never claims the sum-form — so the overstatement was WD-5's compression, not the encyclopedia's &#124; Derive **T6b's pigeonhole step** from &#96;grammar_collision_of_card_lt&#96;; derive the **capacity count** from &#96;ENC-0774&#96;'s prose, and do not describe it as mechanized. | 005_finite_witness_history_integral/claim_ledger.md::WDP005-C19 | WDP005-C19 | 005_finite_witness_history_integral/claim_ledger.md:87 | &#96;&#91;INTERPRETIVE&#93;&#96; | None — this is a reading of what §4 does and does not define, not a theorem. §4 supplies only $w:\Omega\to R$ and the coarse $\bigoplus$; acquiring &#96;ENC-1135&#96;'s notion would require declaring a readout functional, which is **step 8** and belongs to WD-6 | NOT_CARRIED | — | Historical source-path occurrence retained for provenance; no statement-level equivalence to a canonical row is asserted, and no historical status is inherited. | &#124; WDP005-C19 &#124; **WD-5 has no notion of interference other than the one T5 supplies.** The paper declares **no readout, intensity, or probability functional on weights**; its only notion is *destructive interference := vanishing of the total weight of a coarse-graining class*, $\bigoplus_{\gamma\in\mathcal C} w(\gamma) = 0_R$ with some $w(\gamma)\neq 0_R$. &#96;ENC-1135&#96;'s interference is a **different object** — the quadratic readout $\mathcal I(c) = \lvert\sum_s c_s\rvert^{2}$ on a declared $\mathbb C/U(1)$ amplitude carrier, whose $\ell^{1}$ layer is the diagonal Born weights $\lvert c_s\rvert^{2}$. **Neither notion is the other's** &#124; &#96;&#91;INTERPRETIVE&#93;&#96; &#124; None — this is a reading of what §4 does and does not define, not a theorem. §4 supplies only $w:\Omega\to R$ and the coarse $\bigoplus$; acquiring &#96;ENC-1135&#96;'s notion would require declaring a readout functional, which is **step 8** and belongs to WD-6 &#124; Never write bare "interference" for "vanishing of $\bigoplus$", and never drop the qualifier *destructive*: §1's payoff sentence and §13's stop-condition-5 gloss both did, which is the defect this row records. It is **not** sufficient to say "non-computability $\neq$ non-cancellation" — the two notions of interference are also different. §6 T5 fence (iv) &#124; |

The "$\mathbb{Z}_n$ shadow" is docstring vocabulary. D2b's mechanized content is therefore **one continuum identity of which $\Delta\alpha = \pi$ is a numerical instance** — weaker than "the $n = 2$ sample inside an imported complex carrier", and the fence is correspondingly stronger. ## Audited derivation supplement DP-1..DP-9 (2026-10-06)

These rows record the nine audited derivation proposals added to Appendix P
on 2026-10-06 (P.1.a, P.1.b, P.2.a, P.4.a, P.5.a, P.6.a, P.19.a, P.19.b,
P.19.c). They are a supplement outside the canonical table above. They do not
change any canonical claim ID, status or count, and they promote no claim.
Status words: *proved* means a written proof in the manuscript, with the named
finite replay or Lean evidence where stated; *conditional* means proved under
imported or named premises; *refuted* means a counterexample or an argument
in the manuscript; *open* means an unresolved residual. A finite replay
records exact rational checks on the stated examples. It is not a proof of
the general statement. The Lean evidence is the per-component axiom report
named in each section.

| Supplement ID | Statement and status | Evidence and limit |
|---|---|---|
| WDP-DP1-01 | For finite total tables `p:X→A` and `q:X→B`, exactly one holds: a decoder `D:p(X)→B` exists, and is unique on `p(X)`; or a collision exists. `X=∅` is included. **Proved.** | P.1.a written proof; the `dp1_` replay checks; Lean decoder/collision theorems named in P.1.a. Native: `omega_category_theory::finite_report_query`. |
| WDP-DP1-02 | DP-1′: uniqueness holds on `p(X)` only. Nothing is asserted about existence or uniqueness on all of `A`. **Proved (correction).** | P.1.a countermodels 1 and 2. Countermodel 2 is replay only, not stated in Lean (open residual). |
| WDP-DP2-01 | `d_E` is a pseudometric on `S/E` iff `E⊆Z`, and a metric iff `E=Z`. With at least two classes, the least positive class distance `Δ` is attained. If `2ε<Δ`, the class-valued query factors through the report. **Proved.** | P.1.b written proof; the `dp2_` replay checks. Native: `omega_category_theory::finite_section_separation` (cap 512 points). The legacy `Layer4CsQ::evaluate` remains a screen, not a certificate (open residual). |
| WDP-DP3-01 | `Γquot≤Γraw`. Pointwise equality (G) is equivalent to `DᵀK_w=DᵀB_w` and to the sign-vertex condition (GV). The zero tests agree iff `im D∩R2={0}`; (G) implies this, and the converse fails. For a nonempty weight vector, the weight-equivalence constants are `min w` and `max w`. **Proved.** | P.2.a written proof; the `dp3_` replay checks. Lean covers claim 1 and Example 1 only. Native: `omega_functional::gamma_reconciliation` and `omega_solvers::gamma_reconciliation_search` (16-row vertex cap; refusal above). |
| WDP-DP3-02 | DP-3′: `R2={0}` implies (G). The unit-weight example shows (G) with `R2≠{0}`. With weights `(1,3)` that example fails (G): `Γraw(1)=4` and `Γquot(1)=2`. **Proved (correction).** | P.2.a Example 2, finite replay only. |
| WDP-DP4-01 | The idempotents onto $R$ are the maps $C_H=B(U+HQ)$, with $L=I-C_H$ and $\Phi_R([x])\le\lVert Lx\rVert\le\lVert L\rVert\Phi_R([x])$. If $R\ne V$, contractivity is equivalent to $\lVert L\rVert=1$ and existence is a rational LP. If $R=V$, the separate solution is $L=0$. In unit-weight `Q³` with the diagonal line, every projection has `‖L‖≥4/3`, with equality attained, so no contractive complement exists. Intertwining holds iff images and kernels are carried. **Proved.** | P.4.a written proof; the `dp4_` replay checks. Lean covers item 5 and the planar example only. Native: `omega_functional::residual_splitting`, `omega_solvers::rational_feasibility`, `omega_solvers::contractive_complement`; `BudgetExhausted` is a refusal. |
| WDP-DP4-02 | Every codimension-one subspace has a contractive complement for every positive weight vector, including every line in weighted `ℓ1²`. **Proved (written proof added in this pass).** | P.4.a item 6. Exact replay covers four samples only, and there is no Lean statement (open residual). |
| WDP-DP4-03 | The `omega_wdp` projection bounds call the contractive-complement decision. **Open.** | Not wired; see P.4.a residual. |
| WDP-DP6-01 | Finite function sections on declared subsets glue uniquely iff they agree on pairwise overlaps. A global report on `X` additionally needs `U=X`. For a general finite presheaf, the extensions of a matching family are a fiber of the restriction map, of any size. **Proved.** | P.5.a written proof; the `dp6_` replay checks. Native: `omega_sheaf_theory::finite_report_gluing` (64-point carrier cap). The legacy equalizer routine still skips empty intersections (open residual). |
| WDP-DP5-01 | DP-5′ covers four results: the finite midpoint bound; nested closed bounded real intervals share a point; the finite rational intersection criterion; and an exact bisection square-root enclosure, which reaches width `≤δ` within the least `n` with `2^nδ≥max(1,a)`. **Proved.** | P.6.a written proof; the `dp5_` replay checks. Native: `omega_kernel::square_root_enclosure`. Item 2 is not in Lean. |
| WDP-DP5-02 | The requested "nested-without-common-target countermodel", read as a nested family of nonempty, closed, bounded real intervals with empty intersection. **Refuted.** | P.6.a correction. A finite prefix certifies no modulus for later indices (P.6.a item 5). |

## Thermodynamic-limit supplement O.12 (2026-10-07)

These rows record manuscript section O.12, the unique Euclidean
thermodynamic limit of the Meron row-sum covariance. Like the DP supplement
above, they sit outside the canonical table. They change no canonical claim
ID, status or count, and promote no claim. The analytic rows are written
proofs that import the Kotecký–Preiss polymer criterion. The exact rational
CAR checks cover finite rings and chains only. The Lean module states only
rational constants.

| Supplement ID | Statement and status | Evidence and limit |
|---|---|---|
| WDP-TL-01 | Uniformly in even `L≥4` and `u∈[0,1]`, on `\|θ\|≤1/96`: `\|C^L_{0y}(u)\|≤M_1(96\|θ\|)^{d_L(0,y)}`, with `M_1=24576/43`. The tail `sup_L sup_u Σ_y e^{μd}\|C\|≤M_1(1+q)/(1−q)` holds for `μ≥0` and `q=96\|θ\|e^μ<1`. **Proved (written; KP imported).** | O.12, TL-1 and TL-2. Exact vanishing order equals distance on chains and on `L=4,6` rings. The signed-ratio counterexample at `θ=-1/96` is retained; that endpoint has corrected `q=1` and is excluded from the weighted bound. |
| WDP-TL-02 | The open-chain two-point function `C^∞` exists, does not depend on the exhaustion, and is translation invariant. **Proved (written).** | TL-3: intervals containing `[-R,R]` differ by `≤2M_1(96\|θ\|)^{R+1}`. |
| WDP-TL-03 | For the physical periodic CAR ring, the ring kernel minus its wrapped line images vanishes below order `L` and is `≤4M_1(128\|θ\|)^L` on `\|θ\|≤1/128`. The same holds for the sign-erased (spin-periodic) ring. **Proved (written).** | TL-4. Exact lift checks: `L=6,8` through order 4; `L=4` through order 3, differing at 4. Per-site physical/erased agreement below order `L` on `L=4,6`. |
| WDP-TL-04 | `sup_u\|k_{L,θ}−k_θ\|≤4M_1L(128θ)^L→0` on `0≤θ<1/128`, uniformly on `[0,1/192]`. For `μ≥0` and `q<1`: `‖E_L−C^∞‖_{ℓ¹_μ}≤M_1q^{L/2}(4L+4/(1−q))` on `0≤θ≤1/192`. The limit is unique. **Proved (written).** | TL-4 and TL-7. `test_embedding_parts_add_up`. The round-1 constant `7L+2/(1−q)` is superseded, and `μ<0` is excluded. |
| WDP-TL-05 | `k̂_θ(0)=χ(θ)` and `k_0=1/4`. `k_θ(u)=k_θ(1−u)`. `k_θ(u)>0` for `θ<43/56631360`. Every mode is `≥0`. **Proved (written).** | TL-5. Exact zero mode and `u↦1−u` symmetry on `L=4,6,8`. Nonnegativity is a limit of O.11 and is not strict positivity. |
| WDP-TL-06 | For `0<θ≤θ_*=2537/100281465600` and `m≠0`: `k̂_θ(m)≥θ²/(16π²m²)`, for every even `L` and for the limit. Strict positivity alone holds on `0<θ<2θ_*`. **Proved (written).** | TL-6. Exact integration-by-parts identity and `‖B_0‖²≤3` certificate. Lean checks the rational constants only. |
| WDP-TL-07 | Strict positivity of every nonzero integer time mode on `0<θ≤1/192`, with lower bound `θ²/(36π²m²)`, under the imported polymer criterion for the limit. **Conditional written result.** | O.12 and TL-10–TL-12: open-chain SU(2), the Gibbs variational estimate and the local commutator-moment bound. The old uniform-g route is not required. Finite checks and rational Lean constants do not formalize the analytic limit. |

## Lane E character/presentation supplement (2026-10-08)

AuthorityEffect: None. This supplement shows under the declared metric
presentation assumptions the results below. It adds evidence rows without
changing canonical claim IDs or counts. The
[derivation](docs/lane_e_character_duality_completion.md) states all hypotheses
and binds the actual CS/CS_Q/Q source maps. `PROSE_PROOF` labels a written
argument; neither that label nor finite replay asserts Lean formalization.

| Supplement ID | Statement and status | Evidence and limit |
| --- | --- | --- |
| WDP-LE-01 | Algebraic characters of the dyadic colimit are indexed by Z_2; PC in the circle metric holds exactly for integer indices. **PROSE_PROOF.** | LE-1 constructs a discontinuity sequence from infinitely many binary digit changes for every noninteger index. The -1/3 exact witnesses are executable examples, not an enumeration of Z_2. |
| WDP-LE-02 | The metric dyadic colimit has compact-open dual discrete Z and bidual T. Evaluation is its dense proper inclusion, missing [1/3]; the carrier is not locally compact. **PROSE_PROOF.** | LE-2 checks the compact set K and the failed local-compactness hypothesis directly. The discrete topology on the same algebraic group has a different dual. |
| WDP-LE-03 | The circle completion is compact Hausdorff abelian; every PC character extends uniquely and its evaluation into its bidual is a topological isomorphism. **PROSE_PROOF.** | LE-3 uses density, PC-1, and the explicit integer-character classification. It supplies no physical field-theory limit. |
| WDP-LE-04 | For compatible homomorphic finite presentations in a complete invariant metric group, PC is equivalent to unique continuous character extension to H = closure(p(G)); if H is locally compact, classical LCA duality and separation apply. **CONDITIONAL_PROSE_PROOF.** | LE-4 factors through G/ker p, names the imported Pontryagin-van Kampen theorem, and identifies the exact common kernel of PC characters. It does not infer local compactness from completeness or native finite checks. |
| WDP-LE-05 | Relative Lipschitz control gives PC; PC characters extend uniquely as homomorphisms to the completion of the actual image; the real dyadic subtype is incomplete. **SELECTED_LEAN.** | [PresentationDuality](lean4/WitnessedDifference/PresentationDuality.lean) and its [eight-declaration axiom report](lean4/WitnessedDifference/PresentationDualityAxiomReport.lean). Circle uses the chordal metric. Real dyadics approach sqrt(2); this is separate from LE-2's circle-quotient argument. Compact-open classification and general LCA duality are not formalized here. |

The [exact executable](../../007_finite_to_continuum/code/presentation_duality.py)
and [35 focused tests](../../007_finite_to_continuum/tests/test_presentation_duality.py)
cover integer-character distances at levels 0–8, constructed -1/3 refusals,
scaled-third Cauchy prefixes in supplied balls, Fourier aliases and exact
triangle-wave quadrature. Infinite assertions retain the written arguments.
